Abstract: Efficiently training quadruped robot navigation in densely cluttered environments remains a significant challenge. Existing methods are either limited by a lack of safety and agility in simple obstacle distributions or suffer from slow locomotion in complex environments, often requiring excessively long training phases. To this end, we propose SEA-Nav (Safe, Efficient, and Agile Navigation), a reinforcement learning framework for quadruped navigation. Within diverse and dense obstacle environments, a differentiable control barrier function (CBF)-based shield constraints the navigation policy to output safe velocity commands. An adaptive collision replay mechanism and hazardous exploration rewards are introduced to increase the probability of learning from critical experiences, guiding efficient exploration and exploitation. Finally, kinematic action constraints are incorporated to ensure safe velocity commands, facilitating successful physical deployment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach that achieves highly challenging quadruped navigation in the real world with minute-level training time.

</details>


### [189] [Receptogenesis in a Vascularized Robotic Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09473)
*Kadri-Ann Pankratov,Leonid Zinatullin,Hans Priks,Adele Metsniit,Urmas Johanson,Tarmo Tamm,Alvo Aabloo,Edoardo Sinibaldi,Indrek Must*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人通过流体系统在运行时合成新材料硬件，实现物理自适应生长


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化系统依赖预先集成的离散组件，无法实现运行时的物理自适应。受生物循环系统启发，希望通过动态材料重构实现机器人本征功能的物理适应与发展

Method: 采用血管化机器人复合材料，通过流体输送前驱体与外部局部UV照射协调，驱动原位光聚合反应，从内向外化学重构血管系统，将含光潜伏引发剂的前驱体转化为UV敏感聚吡咯固体分散体

Result: 成功实现"受体生成" - 根据环境线索从内部流体储备按需构建传感器，电阻抗特征性下降验证了传感模式，新合成的传感器能闭环控制飞蛾仿生机器人的翅膀拍打

Conclusion: 建立了基于材料的构成演化框架，使机器人能够在复杂环境中物理生长支持新兴行为所需的硬件，为自主系统生成专门特征（如神经血管系统）提供了途径

Abstract: Equipping robotic systems with the capacity to generate $\textit{ex novo}$ hardware during operation extends control of physical adaptability. Unlike modular systems that rely on discrete component integration pre- or post-deployment, we envision the possibility that physical adaptation and development emerge from dynamic material restructuring to shape the body's intrinsic functions. Drawing inspiration from circulatory systems that redistribute mass and function in biological organisms, we utilize fluidics to restructure the material interface, a capability currently unpaired in robotics. Here, we realize this synthetic growth capability through a vascularized robotic composite designed for programmable material synthesis, demonstrated via receptogenesis - the on-demand construction of sensors from internal fluid reserves based on environmental cues. By coordinating the fluidic transport of precursors with external localized UV irradiation, we drive an $\textit{in situ}$ photopolymerization that chemically reconstructs the vasculature from the inside out. This reaction converts precursors with photolatent initiator into a solid dispersion of UV-sensitive polypyrrole, establishing a sensing modality validated by a characteristic decrease in electrical impedance. The newly synthesized sensor closed a control loop to regulate wing flapping in a moth-inspired robotic demonstrator. This physical update increased the robot's capability in real time. This work establishes a materials-based framework for constitutive evolution, enabling robots to physically grow the hardware needed to support emerging behaviors in a complex environment; for example, suggesting a pathway toward autonomous systems capable of generating specialized features, such as neurovascular systems in situated robotics.

</details>


### [190] [StyleVLA: Driving Style-Aware Vision Language Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09482)
*Yuan Gao,Dengyuan Hua,Mattia Piccinini,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Korbinian Moller,Lin Li,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StyleVLA是一个物理感知的视觉语言动作模型，用于生成多样且物理可行的驾驶行为，通过混合损失函数和大规模数据集训练，在自动驾驶任务上超越了闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要生成通用的避撞轨迹，缺乏对多样化驾驶风格（如运动型、舒适型）的适应能力，且将轨迹生成视为简单的token预测，可能产生运动学上不可行的动作。

Method: 提出StyleVLA框架，引入混合损失函数结合运动学一致性约束和连续回归头以提高轨迹可行性。基于Qwen3-VL-4B构建，使用包含1.2k场景、76k BEV样本和42k FPV样本的大规模指令数据集，涵盖五种驾驶风格。

Result: 4B参数的StyleVLA显著优于专有模型（如Gemini-3-Pro）和最先进的VLA模型。在综合驾驶评分（成功率、物理可行性、风格遵循）上，BEV得分为0.55，FPV为0.51，而Gemini-3-Pro分别为0.32和0.35。

Conclusion: 专业化的、物理感知的轻量级模型可以在特定领域任务上超越闭源模型，为个性化自动驾驶提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) bridge visual perception and linguistic reasoning. In Autonomous Driving (AD), this synergy has enabled Vision Language Action (VLA) models, which translate high-level multimodal understanding into driving behaviors, typically represented as future trajectories. However, existing VLA models mainly generate generic collision-free trajectories. Beyond collision avoidance, adapting to diverse driving styles (e.g., sporty, comfortable) is essential for personalized driving. Moreover, many methods treat trajectory generation as naive token prediction, which can produce kinematically infeasible actions. To address these limitations, we present StyleVLA, a physics-informed VLA framework for generating diverse and physically plausible driving behaviors. We introduce a hybrid loss that combines a kinematic consistency constraint with a continuous regression head to improve trajectory feasibility. To train StyleVLA, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, we construct a large-scale instruction dataset with over 1.2k scenarios, 76k Bird's Eye View (BEV) samples, and 42k First Person View (FPV) samples, with ground-truth trajectories for five driving styles and natural-language instructions. Experiments show that our 4B-parameter StyleVLA significantly outperforms proprietary models (e.g., Gemini-3-Pro) and state-of-the-art VLA models. Using a composite driving score measuring success rate, physical feasibility, and style adherence, StyleVLA achieves 0.55 on BEV and 0.51 on FPV, versus 0.32 and 0.35 for Gemini-3-Pro. These results show that a specialized, physics-informed, lightweight model can surpass closed-source models on domain-specific tasks.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Short-Horizon: VQ-Memory for Robust Long-Horizon Manipulation in Non-Markovian Simulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09513)
*Wang Honghui,Jing Zhi,Ao Jicong,Song Shiji,Li Xuelong,Huang Gao,Bai Chenjia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RuleSafe是一个基于LLM辅助仿真框架的铰接操作基准，包含多种解锁机制的安全箱任务，需要多阶段推理和操作策略。论文提出VQ-Memory，一种使用VQ-VAE编码过去本体感觉状态的紧凑结构化时间表示，能提升长时程规划和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真基准大多集中于简单的拾取放置任务，未能捕捉真实世界任务的非马尔可夫特性和铰接物体交互的复杂性。真实机器人数据收集成本高昂，需要更复杂的仿真基准来评估和生成数据。

Method: 1) 提出RuleSafe基准：基于LLM生成规则的安全箱解锁任务，包含钥匙锁、密码锁、逻辑锁等多种解锁机制，产生非马尔可夫和长时程任务。2) 提出VQ-Memory：使用向量量化变分自编码器(VQ-VAE)将过去本体感觉状态编码为离散潜在令牌，形成紧凑结构化时间表示，过滤低级噪声同时保留高级任务阶段上下文。

Result: 在先进的视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)和扩散策略上的大量实验表明，VQ-Memory能持续改进长时程规划，增强对未见配置的泛化能力，并以更低的计算成本实现更高效的操作。

Conclusion: RuleSafe基准和VQ-Memory表示方法为解决机器人仿真中的非马尔可夫和长时程任务提供了有效方案，VQ-Memory的紧凑结构化时间表示能与现有VLA模型兼容，显著提升性能。

Abstract: The high cost of collecting real-robot data has made robotic simulation a scalable platform for both evaluation and data generation. Yet most existing benchmarks concentrate on simple manipulation tasks such as pick-and-place, failing to capture the non-Markovian characteristics of real-world tasks and the complexity of articulated object interactions. To address this limitation, we present RuleSafe, a new articulated manipulation benchmark built upon a scalable LLM-aided simulation framework. RuleSafe features safes with diverse unlocking mechanisms, such as key locks, password locks, and logic locks, which require different multi-stage reasoning and manipulation strategies. These LLM-generated rules produce non-Markovian and long-horizon tasks that require temporal modeling and memory-based reasoning. We further propose VQ-Memory, a compact and structured temporal representation that uses vector-quantized variational autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) to encode past proprioceptive states into discrete latent tokens. This representation filters low-level noise while preserving high-level task-phase context, providing lightweight yet robust temporal cues that are compatible with existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLA). Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art VLA models and diffusion policies show that VQ-Memory consistently improves long-horizon planning, enhances generalization to unseen configurations, and enables more efficient manipulation with reduced computational cost. Project page: vqmemory.github.io

</details>


### [192] [NS-VLA: Towards Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09542)
*Ziyue Zhu,Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Zhiqiu Zhao,Shengjie Li,Yi Wang,Fang Li,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经符号视觉语言动作（NS-VLA）框架，通过在线强化学习解决VLA模型在可重用原语学习、数据依赖和探索能力方面的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型面临三个主要挑战：1）难以学习相关且可重用的原语；2）过度依赖大规模数据和复杂架构；3）难以超越演示数据进行探索。需要一种更高效、更具探索性的框架

Method: 提出NS-VLA框架：1）使用符号编码器嵌入视觉和语言特征并提取结构化原语；2）利用符号求解器进行数据高效的动作序列生成；3）通过在线强化学习优化生成，实现扩展性探索

Result: 在机器人操作基准测试中，NS-VLA在单次训练和数据扰动设置下均优于先前方法，同时展现出优越的零样本泛化能力、高数据效率和扩展的探索空间

Conclusion: NS-VLA框架通过神经符号结合和在线强化学习，有效解决了VLA模型的关键挑战，为机器人操作提供了更高效、更具泛化能力的解决方案

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are formulated to ground instructions in visual context and generate action sequences for robotic manipulation. Despite recent progress, VLA models still face challenges in learning related and reusable primitives, reducing reliance on large-scale data and complex architectures, and enabling exploration beyond demonstrations. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action (NS-VLA) framework via online reinforcement learning (RL). It introduces a symbolic encoder to embedding vision and language features and extract structured primitives, utilizes a symbolic solver for data-efficient action sequencing, and leverages online RL to optimize generation via expansive exploration. Experiments on robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that NS-VLA outperforms previous methods in both one-shot training and data-perturbed settings, while simultaneously exhibiting superior zero-shot generalizability, high data efficiency and expanded exploration space. Our code is available.

</details>


### [193] [On the Cost of Evolving Task Specialization in Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09552)
*Paolo Leopardi,Heiko Hamann,Jonas Kuckling,Tanja Katharina Kaiser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过进化神经网络探索机器人群体中任务专业化的成本效益分析，发现在有限优化预算下，通用行为比任务专业化行为表现更好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务专业化可以简化机器人行为并提高多机器人系统效率，但先前研究主要关注可行性而非成本。本研究旨在对机器人群体中的任务专业化进行成本效益分析

Method: 使用觅食场景，在有限评估预算下进化人工神经网络：一种作为整个任务的通用行为，另一种作为子任务的任务专业化行为

Result: 通用行为可以成功优化，而进化的任务专业化控制器无法有效合作，表现比通用行为更差。任务专业化在有限优化预算下不一定能提高效率

Conclusion: 任务专业化并不总是能提高效率，特别是在有限优化预算下。通用行为可能比任务专业化行为表现更好，这挑战了任务专业化必然带来效率提升的假设

Abstract: Task specialization can lead to simpler robot behaviors and higher efficiency in multi-robot systems. Previous works have shown the emergence of task specialization during evolutionary optimization, focusing on feasibility rather than costs. In this study, we take first steps toward a cost-benefit analysis of task specialization in robot swarms using a foraging scenario. We evolve artificial neural networks as generalist behaviors for the entire task and as task-specialist behaviors for subtasks within a limited evaluation budget. We show that generalist behaviors can be successfully optimized while the evolved task-specialist controllers fail to cooperate efficiently, resulting in worse performance than the generalists. Consequently, task specialization does not necessarily improve efficiency when optimization budget is limited.

</details>


### [194] [Trajectory Optimization for Self-Wrap-Aware Cable-Towed Planar Object Manipulation under Implicit Tension Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09557)
*Yu Li,Amin Fakhari,Hamid Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于电缆拖曳操纵的轨迹优化方法，特别关注电缆自缠绕现象，通过松弛层次结构处理张紧/松弛混合模式切换问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电缆/绳索在可变形物体操纵中普遍存在，作为力传递介质，其路径和接触决定了力矩传递方式。在电缆拖曳操纵中，传递是单向和混合的：电缆只有在拉紧时才能传递拉力，松弛时则无力；实践中电缆还会接触物体边界并自缠绕边缘，这不仅涉及碰撞避免，更改变了力矩传递通道，通过改变有效作用点和力臂，将路径几何与刚体运动和张紧耦合起来。

Method: 将自缠绕拖曳建模为路径感知、张紧隐式的轨迹优化问题，耦合了：(i) 张紧隐式的拉紧/松弛约束；(ii) 路径条件化的有效长度和力矩传递映射。构建了从严格模式条件参考到三个可处理松弛的层次结构：全模式松弛、二模式松弛和隐式模式松弛。

Result: 在平面拖曳任务中，将路径作为显式决策通常产生保守解，保持在切换边界附近；而隐式模式松弛通过状态演化诱导自缠绕，并在需要转向时利用重定向的扭矩通道。

Conclusion: 电缆自缠绕拖曳需要耦合路径几何与刚体运动的优化方法，隐式模式松弛方法能够有效处理混合模式切换，利用电缆接触和缠绕带来的力矩传递优势，实现更高效的操纵。

Abstract: Cable/rope elements are pervasive in deformable-object manipulation, often serving as a deformable force-transmission medium whose routing and contact determine how wrenches are delivered. In cable-towed manipulation, transmission is unilateral and hybrid: the tether can pull only when taut and becomes force-free when slack; in practice, the tether may also contact the object boundary and self-wrap around edges, which is not merely collision avoidance but a change of the wrench transmission channel by shifting the effective application point and moment arm, thereby coupling routing geometry with rigid-body motion and tensioning. We formulate self-wrap towing as a routing-aware, tensioning-implicit trajectory optimization (TITO) problem that couples (i) a tensioning-implicit taut/slack constraint and (ii) routing-conditioned transmission maps for effective length and wrench, and we build a relaxation hierarchy from a strict mode-conditioned reference to three tractable relaxations: Full-Mode Relaxation (FMR), Binary-Mode Relaxation (BMR), and Implicit-Mode Relaxation (IMR). Across planar towing tasks, we find that making routing an explicit decision often yields conservative solutions that stay near switching boundaries, whereas IMR induces self-wrap through state evolution and exploits the redirected torque channel whenever turning requires it.

</details>


### [195] [ReTac-ACT: A State-Gated Vision-Tactile Fusion Transformer for Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09565)
*Minchi Ruan,LiangQing Zhou,Hongtong Li,Zongtao Wang,ZhaoMing Lu,Jianwei Zhang,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReTac-ACT：一种视觉-触觉模仿学习策略，通过双向交叉注意力、动态触觉门控和触觉重建目标，解决精密装配中的视觉遮挡问题，在NIST装配任务板上实现90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密装配需要在"最后一毫米"接触密集区域进行亚毫米级校正，但视觉反馈因末端执行器和工件的遮挡而失效，需要触觉信息来补充。

Method: 提出ReTac-ACT策略，包含三个协同机制：1）双向交叉注意力实现视觉-触觉特征相互增强；2）本体感知条件门控网络在视觉遮挡时动态提升触觉依赖；3）触觉重建目标强制学习与操作相关的接触信息而非通用视觉纹理。

Result: 在标准化NIST装配任务板M1基准测试中，ReTac-ACT实现90%的孔插入成功率，显著优于纯视觉和通用基线方法，在工业级0.1mm间隙下仍保持80%成功率。消融研究验证每个架构组件都不可或缺。

Conclusion: ReTac-ACT通过创新的视觉-触觉融合机制有效解决了精密装配中的视觉遮挡问题，代码库和包含视觉-触觉特征的数据集将开源以支持可重复研究。

Abstract: Precision assembly requires sub-millimeter corrections in contact-rich "last-millimeter" regions where visual feedback fails due to occlusion from the end-effector and workpiece. We present ReTac-ACT (Reconstruction-enhanced Tactile ACT), a vision-tactile imitation learning policy that addresses this challenge through three synergistic mechanisms: (i) bidirectional cross-attention enabling reciprocal visuo-tactile feature enhancement before fusion, (ii) a proprioception-conditioned gating network that dynamically elevates tactile reliance when visual occlusion occurs, and (iii) a tactile reconstruction objective enforcing learning of manipulation-relevant contact information rather than generic visual textures. Evaluated on the standardized NIST Assembly Task Board M1 benchmark, ReTac-ACT achieves 90% peg-in-hole success, substantially outperforming vision-only and generalist baseline methods, and maintains 80% success at industrial-grade 0.1mm clearance. Ablation studies validate that each architectural component is indispensable. The ReTac-ACT codebase and a vision-tactile demonstration dataset covering various clearance levels with both visual and tactile features will be released to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [196] [SCDP: Learning Humanoid Locomotion from Partial Observations via Mixed-Observation Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09574)
*Milo Carroll,Tianhu Peng,Lingfan Bao,Chengxu Zhou,Zhibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCDP是一种仅使用板载传感器实现人形机器人运动控制的方法，无需显式状态估计，通过混合观测训练将感知与监督解耦，在仿真和真实机器人上都表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动控制方法依赖于需要复杂且不可靠状态估计的特权全身状态，这限制了实际部署。需要开发仅使用板载传感器就能实现鲁棒运动控制的方法。

Method: 提出传感器条件扩散策略（SCDP）：1）混合观测训练：扩散模型以传感器历史为条件，但被监督预测特权未来状态-动作轨迹；2）受限去噪、上下文分布对齐和上下文感知注意力掩码，鼓励模型内部进行隐式状态估计并防止训练-部署不匹配。

Result: 在仿真中，SCDP在速度控制任务上达到99-100%成功率，在AMASS测试集上达到93%跟踪成功率，性能与使用特权信息的基线相当。成功在真实G1人形机器人上以50Hz频率部署，无需外部传感或状态估计。

Conclusion: SCDP证明了仅使用板载传感器就能实现鲁棒的人形机器人运动控制，消除了对显式状态估计的依赖，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Distilling humanoid locomotion control from offline datasets into deployable policies remains a challenge, as existing methods rely on privileged full-body states that require complex and often unreliable state estimation. We present Sensor-Conditioned Diffusion Policies (SCDP) that enables humanoid locomotion using only onboard sensors, eliminating the need for explicit state estimation. SCDP decouples sensing from supervision through mixed-observation training: diffusion model conditions on sensor histories while being supervised to predict privileged future state-action trajectories, enforcing the model to infer the motion dynamics under partial observability. We further develop restricted denoising, context distribution alignment, and context-aware attention masking to encourage implicit state estimation within the model and to prevent train-deploy mismatch. We validate SCDP on velocity-commanded locomotion and motion reference tracking tasks. In simulation, SCDP achieves near-perfect success on velocity control (99-100%) and 93% tracking success in AMASS test set, performing comparable to privileged baselines while using only onboard sensors. Finally, we deploy the trained policy on a real G1 humanoid at 50 Hz, demonstrating robust real robot locomotion without external sensing or state estimation.

</details>


### [197] [Towards Terrain-Aware Safe Locomotion for Quadrupedal Robots Using Proprioceptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09585)
*Peiyu Yang,Jiatao Ding,Wei Pan,Claudio Semini,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体感知的四足机器人安全控制框架，通过2.5维地形估计和CBF控制屏障函数，在不依赖外部传感器的情况下实现地形感知的安全运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低成本四足机器人在不平坦地形上仅使用本体感知传感器（IMU、关节编码器、接触力传感器）时的安全运动问题，现有方法在可靠估计和安全控制方面仍有挑战

Method: 1) 提出估计框架：生成2.5维地形图并提取支撑面参数，集成到接触和状态估计中；2) 将估计框架集成到安全关键控制管道中：通过控制屏障函数(CBF)提供严格安全保证

Result: 地形估计方法提供平滑地形表示；耦合估计框架相比解耦框架：基座位置估计平均绝对误差降低64.8%，估计方差减少47.2%，接触估计鲁棒性提高；地形感知CBF确保全局安全（避开危险区域）和局部安全（防止身体-地形碰撞）

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种仅依赖本体感知的四足机器人安全运动框架，通过耦合地形、状态和接触估计，结合CBF控制，在不平坦地形上实现了可靠的安全保证

Abstract: Achieving safe quadrupedal locomotion in real-world environments has attracted much attention in recent years. When walking over uneven terrain, achieving reliable estimation and realising safety-critical control based on the obtained information is still an open question. To address this challenge, especially for low-cost robots equipped solely with proprioceptive sensors (e.g., IMUs, joint encoders, and contact force sensors), this work first presents an estimation framework that generates a 2.5-D terrain map and extracts support plane parameters, which are then integrated into contact and state estimation. Then, we integrate this estimation framework into a safety-critical control pipeline by formulating control barrier functions that provide rigorous safety guarantees. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed terrain estimation method provides smooth terrain representations. Moreover, the coupled estimation framework of terrain, state, and contact reduces the mean absolute error of base position estimation by 64.8%, decreases the estimation variance by 47.2%, and improves the robustness of contact estimation compared to a decoupled framework. The terrain-informed CBFs integrate historical terrain information and current proprioceptive measurements to ensure global safety by keeping the robot out of hazardous areas and local safety by preventing body-terrain collision, relying solely on proprioceptive sensing.

</details>


### [198] [A Generalized Voronoi Graph based Coverage Control Approach for Non-Convex Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09596)
*Zuyi Guo,Ronghao Zheng,Meiqin Liu,Senlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于广义Voronoi图的多机器人覆盖控制方法，包含负载均衡和协作覆盖两阶段，用于非凸区域高效覆盖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人系统在包含多个障碍物的非凸区域中高效覆盖的挑战

Method: 基于广义Voronoi图(GVG)的覆盖控制方法，包含两个阶段：1)负载均衡算法阶段：基于GVG将非凸区域划分为多个子区域，开发考虑子区域质量差异的加权负载均衡算法，通过迭代优化机器人分配比例实现负载平衡；2)协作覆盖阶段：每个机器人使用新的控制器有效覆盖区域

Result: 证明了方法的收敛性，并通过仿真评估了性能

Conclusion: 提出的基于GVG的覆盖控制方法能够有效解决多机器人在非凸区域中的覆盖问题，实现负载均衡和高效协作覆盖

Abstract: To address the challenge of efficient coverage by multi-robot systems in non-convex regions with multiple obstacles, this paper proposes a coverage control method based on the Generalized Voronoi Graph (GVG), which has two phases: Load-Balancing Algorithm phase and Collaborative Coverage phase. In Load-Balancing Algorithm phase, the non-convex region is partitioned into multiple sub-regions based on GVG. Besides, a weighted load-balancing algorithm is developed, which considers the quality differences among sub-regions. By iteratively optimizing the robot allocation ratio, the number of robots in each sub-region is matched with the sub-region quality to achieve load balance. In Collaborative Coverage phase, each robot is controlled by a new controller to effectively coverage the region. The convergence of the method is proved and its performance is evaluated through simulations.

</details>


### [199] [DRIFT: Dual-Representation Inter-Fusion Transformer for Automated Driving Perception with 4D Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09695)
*Siqi Pei,Andras Palffy,Dariu M. Gavrila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT提出了一种用于4D雷达点云的双路径架构，通过点路径和柱路径分别捕获局部和全局上下文特征，并通过特征共享层融合，在目标检测和自由道路估计任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达成本低且对恶劣天气鲁棒，但点云密度远低于激光雷达，因此需要有效利用局部和全局上下文信息来提升感知性能。

Method: 提出双路径架构：点路径聚合细粒度局部特征，柱路径编码粗粒度全局特征；通过多阶段特征共享层将两条路径交织，充分利用两种表示。

Result: 在View-of-Delft数据集和内部数据集上评估，在目标检测和自由道路估计任务上优于基线。例如在VoD数据集上达到52.6% mAP，优于CenterPoint的45.4%。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过有效融合局部和全局上下文特征，提升了4D雷达点云的感知性能，为自动驾驶系统提供了更可靠的感知解决方案。

Abstract: 4D radars, which provide 3D point cloud data along with Doppler velocity, are attractive components of modern automated driving systems due to their low cost and robustness under adverse weather conditions. However, they provide a significantly lower point cloud density than LiDAR sensors. This makes it important to exploit not only local but also global contextual scene information. This paper proposes DRIFT, a model that effectively captures and fuses both local and global contexts through a dual-path architecture. The model incorporates a point path to aggregate fine-grained local features and a pillar path to encode coarse-grained global features. These two parallel paths are intertwined via novel feature-sharing layers at multiple stages, enabling full utilization of both representations. DRIFT is evaluated on the widely used View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset and a proprietary internal dataset. It outperforms the baselines on the tasks of object detection and/or free road estimation. For example, DRIFT achieves a mean average precision (mAP) of 52.6\% (compared to, say, 45.4\% of CenterPoint) on the VoD dataset.

</details>


### [200] [Robotic Scene Cloning:Advancing Zero-Shot Robotic Scene Adaptation in Manipulation via Visual Prompt Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09712)
*Binyuan Huang,Yuqing Wen,Yucheng Zhao,Yaosi Hu,Tiancai Wang,Chang Wen Chen,Haoqiang Fan,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC)方法，通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现场景特定适应，提升预训练模型在真实场景的零样本能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人模型在真实用户场景部署困难，零样本能力有限，通常需要大量现场数据收集，需要解决场景适应问题

Method: RSC方法利用视觉提示机制和精心调优的条件注入模块，通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现准确且场景一致的样本生成，不仅能转移纹理还能进行适度的形状适应

Result: 在各种模拟和真实环境实验中，RSC显著提升了策略在目标环境中的泛化能力，在不同物体类型上表现出可靠的任务性能

Conclusion: RSC方法有效解决了预训练机器人模型在真实场景部署的适应问题，通过场景克隆技术实现了更好的零样本泛化性能

Abstract: Modern robots can perform a wide range of simple tasks and adapt to diverse scenarios in the well-trained environment. However, deploying pre-trained robot models in real-world user scenarios remains challenging due to their limited zero-shot capabilities, often necessitating extensive on-site data collection. To address this issue, we propose Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC), a novel method designed for scene-specific adaptation by editing existing robot operation trajectories. RSC achieves accurate and scene-consistent sample generation by leveraging a visual prompting mechanism and a carefully tuned condition injection module. Not only transferring textures but also performing moderate shape adaptations in response to the visual prompts, RSC demonstrates reliable task performance across a variety of object types. Experiments across various simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that RSC significantly enhances policy generalization in target environments.

</details>


### [201] [Let's Reward Step-by-Step: Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment for Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09740)
*Haoyuan Li,Rui Liu,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SACA框架通过感知基础步进感知审计和场景条件分组构建，从失败轨迹中提取密集监督，解决了VLN-CE中强化微调稀疏奖励和模仿学习复合错误的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLN-CE训练范式存在两个主要问题：1) 监督微调(SFT)策略容易产生复合错误，难以从分布外状态恢复；2) 强化微调(RFT)方法受限于稀疏结果奖励，二元反馈无法为单个步骤分配信用，导致梯度信号崩溃。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA)框架：1) Perception-Grounded Step-Aware审计器逐步评估进度，将失败轨迹分解为有效前缀和精确分歧点；2) Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction机制动态路由批次到专门的重新采样和优化策略。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SACA实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SACA通过从非完美轨迹中提取密集监督，有效解决了VLN-CE中训练稳定性和泛化能力的平衡问题，为视觉语言导航提供了更鲁棒的训练框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to learn complex reasoning from long-horizon human interactions. While Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have driven recent progress, current training paradigms struggle to balance generalization capability, error recovery and training stability. Specifically, (i) policies derived from SFT suffer from compounding errors, struggling to recover from out-of-distribution states, and (ii) Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) methods e.g. GRPO are bottlenecked by sparse outcome rewards. Their binary feedback fails to assign credit to individual steps, leading to gradient signal collapse in failure dominant batches. To address these challenges, we introduce Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA), a framework designed to extract dense supervision from imperfect trajectories. At its core, the Perception-Grounded Step-Aware auditor evaluates progress step-by-step, disentangling failed trajectories into valid prefixes and exact divergence points. Leveraging these signals, Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction mechanism dynamically routes batches to specialized resampling and optimization strategies. Extensive experiments on VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate that SACA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [202] [Caterpillar-Inspired Spring-Based Compressive Continuum Robot for Bristle-based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09745)
*Zhixian Hu,Yu She,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于弹簧的肌腱驱动连续体机器人，集成商业机械臂用于受限空间检测，结合弯曲和轴向长度变化，集成人工刚毛接触传感器实现表面感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性机器人在管道、风道等受限空间探索中面临空间有限、几何不规则、访问受限等挑战，需要更灵活、紧凑的解决方案

Method: 设计紧凑的弹簧基肌腱驱动连续体机器人，集成商业机械臂；采用机械柔顺的连续体结构结合肌腱驱动模块，实现弯曲和轴向长度变化的耦合；使用恒定曲率运动学模型进行位置控制；集成人工刚毛接触传感器实现表面感知

Result: 在提出的模型和控制管道下，平均位置误差为4.32毫米；成功演示了表面感知和通过接触交互的受限空间探索

Conclusion: 这种紧凑、柔顺的设计为商业机器人提供了经济有效的升级方案，有望在具有挑战性的环境中实现有效探索

Abstract: Exploration of confined spaces, such as pipelines and ducts, remains challenging for conventional rigid robots due to limited space, irregular geometry, and restricted access. Inspired by caterpillar locomotion and sensing, this paper presents a compact spring-based tendon-driven continuum robot that integrates with commercial robotic arms for confined-space inspection. The system combines a mechanically compliant continuum body with a tendon actuation module, enabling coupled bending and axial length change, and uses a constant-curvature kinematic model for positional control. Experiments show a mean position error of 4.32 mm under the proposed model and control pipeline. To extend the system from motion to inspection, we integrate an artificial bristle contact sensor and demonstrate surface perception and confined-space exploration through contact interactions. This compact and compliant design offers a cost-effective upgrade for commercial robots and promises effective exploration in challenging environments.

</details>


### [203] [MuxGel: Simultaneous Dual-Modal Visuo-Tactile Sensing via Spatially Multiplexing and Deep Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09761)
*Zhixian Hu,Zhengtong Xu,Sheeraz Athar,Juan Wachs,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuxGel是一种新型视觉触觉传感器，通过棋盘格涂层图案同时捕获外部视觉信息和接触触觉信号，解决了传统传感器中不透明涂层阻挡预接触视觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数基于视觉的触觉传感器面临基本权衡：不透明涂层可实现触觉传感但会阻挡预接触视觉，这限制了机器人操作的感知能力。

Method: 采用空间复用设计，使用棋盘格涂层图案，将触觉敏感区域与透明窗口交错排列；开发基于U-Net的重建框架，通过模拟到真实的训练流程，从复用输入中恢复全分辨率视觉和触觉信号。

Result: 实验表明该框架在未见物体上具有良好的泛化性和准确性；在抓取任务中，双模态反馈促进了预接触对齐和接触后交互，增强了现有视觉触觉传感器的感知能力。

Conclusion: MuxGel通过单一摄像头同时捕获视觉和触觉信息，解决了传统传感器的权衡问题，保持了标准外形尺寸和硬件兼容性，提升了机器人操作的感知能力。

Abstract: High-fidelity visuo-tactile sensing is important for precise robotic manipulation. However, most vision-based tactile sensors face a fundamental trade-off: opaque coatings enable tactile sensing but block pre-contact vision. To address this, we propose MuxGel, a spatially multiplexed sensor that captures both external visual information and contact-induced tactile signals through a single camera. By using a checkerboard coating pattern, MuxGel interleaves tactile-sensitive regions with transparent windows for external vision. This design maintains standard form factors, allowing for plug-and-play integration into GelSight-style sensors by simply replacing the gel pad. To recover full-resolution vision and tactile signals from the multiplexed inputs, we develop a U-Net-based reconstruction framework. Leveraging a sim-to-real pipeline, our model effectively decouples and restores high-fidelity tactile and visual fields simultaneously. Experiments on unseen objects demonstrate the framework's generalization and accuracy. Furthermore, we demonstrate MuxGel's utility in grasping tasks, where dual-modality feedback facilitates both pre-contact alignment and post-contact interaction. Results show that MuxGel enhances the perceptual capabilities of existing vision-based tactile sensors while maintaining compatibility with their hardware stacks. Project webpage: https://zhixianhu.github.io/muxgel/.

</details>


### [204] [TIMID: Time-Dependent Mistake Detection in Videos of Robot Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09782)
*Nerea Gallego,Fernando Salanova,Claudio Mannarano,Cristian Mahulea,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TIMID：一种新的视频异常检测架构，专门用于检测机器人执行高级任务时的时间相关错误，通过弱监督训练，在模拟和真实环境中都能有效识别时间错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测（VAD）框架主要关注低级别的运动学或动作失败，难以检测复杂的时空任务违规，因为这些违规不一定表现为低级别执行错误。机器人执行复杂任务序列时，失败方式越来越多，需要能检测高级别时间相关错误的方法。

Method: 提出TIMID架构，接收视频和任务描述及潜在错误提示作为输入，输出逐帧的错误检测预测。采用VAD公式化，只需每个视频一个标签进行弱监督训练。为解决错误执行数据稀缺问题，引入包含受控时间错误的多机器人模拟数据集，支持零样本模拟到真实评估。

Result: 实验表明，现成的视觉语言模型（VLMs）缺乏该任务所需的显式时间推理能力，而TIMID框架能成功检测不同类型的时间错误。通过模拟数据集训练，在真实环境中实现零样本评估。

Conclusion: TIMID架构有效解决了机器人执行高级任务时的时间相关错误检测问题，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据集解决了数据稀缺挑战，相比现有VAD方法和VLMs在时间推理方面表现更优。

Abstract: As robotic systems execute increasingly difficult task sequences, so does the number of ways in which they can fail. Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) frameworks typically focus on singular, low-level kinematic or action failures, struggling to identify more complex temporal or spatial task violations, because they do not necessarily manifest as low-level execution errors. To address this problem, the main contribution of this paper is a new VAD-inspired architecture, TIMID, which is able to detect robot time-dependent mistakes when executing high-level tasks. Our architecture receives as inputs a video and prompts of the task and the potential mistake, and returns a frame-level prediction in the video of whether the mistake is present or not. By adopting a VAD formulation, the model can be trained with weak supervision, requiring only a single label per video. Additionally, to alleviate the problem of data scarcity of incorrect executions, we introduce a multi-robot simulation dataset with controlled temporal errors and real executions for zero-shot sim-to-real evaluation. Our experiments demonstrate that out-of-the-box VLMs lack the explicit temporal reasoning required for this task, whereas our framework successfully detects different types of temporal errors. Project: https://ropertunizar.github.io/TIMID/

</details>


### [205] [Lightweight 3D LiDAR-Based UAV Tracking: An Adaptive Extended Kalman Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09783)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于轻量级LiDAR的无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器框架，能在GPS拒止环境下实现可靠的相对定位，特别适合小型无人机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无人机群需要精确的相对定位来实现协调飞行和避撞。虽然视觉跟踪已被广泛研究，但基于3D LiDAR的方法在变化光照条件下更鲁棒，却未被充分利用。现有系统通常依赖笨重、高功耗的传感器，不适合具有严格载荷和能量限制的小型无人机。

Method: 提出轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器框架。该方法动态调整噪声协方差矩阵（使用创新和残差统计），以处理非重复扫描3D LiDAR产生的稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据。还包含恢复机制，确保在临时检测失败时跟踪连续性。

Result: 在真实飞行场景中使用DJI F550无人机搭载Livox Mid-360 LiDAR进行实验验证。该方法在稀疏LiDAR返回和间歇检测条件下表现出鲁棒的无人机跟踪性能，在激进机动中始终优于标准卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在GPS拒止环境中实现可靠的相对定位，无需多传感器阵列或外部基础设施，特别适合具有严格载荷限制的小型无人机应用。

Abstract: Accurate relative positioning is crucial for swarm aerial robotics, enabling coordinated flight and collision avoidance. Although vision-based tracking has been extensively studied, 3D LiDAR-based methods remain underutilized despite their robustness under varying lighting conditions. Existing systems often rely on bulky, power-intensive sensors, making them impractical for small UAVs with strict payload and energy constraints. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-based UAV tracking system incorporating an Adaptive Extended Kalman Filter (AEKF) framework. Our approach effectively addresses the challenges posed by sparse, noisy, and nonuniform point cloud data generated by non-repetitive scanning 3D LiDARs, ensuring reliable tracking while remaining suitable for small drones with strict payload constraints. Unlike conventional filtering techniques, the proposed method dynamically adjusts the noise covariance matrices using innovation and residual statistics, thereby enhancing tracking accuracy under real-world conditions. Additionally, a recovery mechanism ensures continuity of tracking during temporary detection failures caused by scattered LiDAR returns or occlusions. Experimental validation was performed using a Livox Mid-360 LiDAR mounted on a DJI F550 UAV in real-world flight scenarios. The proposed method demonstrated robust UAV tracking performance under sparse LiDAR returns and intermittent detections, consistently outperforming both standard Kalman filtering and particle filtering approaches during aggressive maneuvers. These results confirm that the framework enables reliable relative positioning in GPS-denied environments without the need for multi-sensor arrays or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [206] [Emerging Extrinsic Dexterity in Cluttered Scenes via Dynamics-aware Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09882)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yifan Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Mi Yan,Yuntian Deng,Xuesong Shi,Xiaoguang Zhao,Yizhou Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPL框架，通过显式世界建模学习接触动力学表示，指导强化学习实现非抓取式操作，在杂乱场景中超越现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对杂乱场景中多物体接触动力学的显式建模，限制了非抓取式操作的实用性和现实世界应用

Method: DAPL框架：通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导的物体动力学表示，用该表示指导强化学习策略，无需手工接触启发式或复杂奖励设计

Result: 在未见过的杂乱场景中比抓取式操作、人工遥操作和现有基于表示的方法成功率高出25%以上；现实世界成功率约50%，杂货部署展示了稳健的模拟到现实迁移

Conclusion: DAPL框架通过显式动力学建模实现了杂乱场景中的非抓取式操作，展示了实用性和现实世界应用潜力

Abstract: Extrinsic dexterity leverages environmental contact to overcome the limitations of prehensile manipulation. However, achieving such dexterity in cluttered scenes remains challenging and underexplored, as it requires selectively exploiting contact among multiple interacting objects with inherently coupled dynamics. Existing approaches lack explicit modeling of such complex dynamics and therefore fall short in non-prehensile manipulation in cluttered environments, which in turn limits their practical applicability in real-world environments. In this paper, we introduce a Dynamics-Aware Policy Learning (DAPL) framework that can facilitate policy learning with a learned representation of contact-induced object dynamics in cluttered environments. This representation is learned through explicit world modeling and used to condition reinforcement learning, enabling extrinsic dexterity to emerge without hand-crafted contact heuristics or complex reward shaping. We evaluate our approach in both simulation and the real world. Our method outperforms prehensile manipulation, human teleoperation, and prior representation-based policies by over 25% in success rate on unseen simulated cluttered scenes with varying densities. The real-world success rate reaches around 50% across 10 cluttered scenes, while a practical grocery deployment further demonstrates robust sim-to-real transfer and applicability.

</details>


### [207] [Robust Cooperative Localization in Featureless Environments: A Comparative Study of DCL, StCL, CCL, CI, and Standard-CL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09886)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了五种协同定位方法，发现CI方法在一致性和准确性之间取得最佳平衡，DCL在挑战性条件下最稳定，而StCL和Standard-CL虽然精度最高但一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境中，多机器人系统的精确定位至关重要。协同定位方法有多种实现方式，但缺乏系统性的比较研究来指导实际应用中的算法选择。

Method: 在ROS中实现了五种协同定位方法：CCL、DCL、StCL、CI和Standard-CL，通过蒙特卡洛仿真在弱数据关联和鲁棒检测两种条件下进行评估。

Result: StCL和Standard-CL定位误差最低但滤波器一致性严重不足；DCL在挑战性条件下最稳定；CI在一致性和准确性之间取得最佳平衡；CCL理论最优但对异常值敏感。

Conclusion: CI方法是最平衡的选择，DCL适合挑战性环境，而StCL和Standard-CL不适合安全关键应用。研究结果为基于应用需求选择协同定位算法提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Cooperative localization (CL) enables accurate position estimation in multi-robot systems operating in GPS-denied environments. This paper presents a comparative study of five CL approaches: Centralized Cooperative Localization (CCL), Decentralized Cooperative Localization (DCL), Sequential Cooperative Localization (StCL), Covariance Intersection (CI), and Standard Cooperative Localization (Standard-CL). All methods are implemented in ROS and evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations under two conditions: weak data association and robust detection. Our analysis reveals fundamental trade-offs among the methods. StCL and Standard-CL achieve the lowest position errors but exhibit severe filter inconsistency, making them unsuitable for safety-critical applications. DCL demonstrates remarkable stability under challenging conditions due to its measurement stride mechanism, which provides implicit regularization against outliers. CI emerges as the most balanced approach, achieving near-optimal consistency while maintaining competitive accuracy. CCL provides theoretically optimal estimation but shows sensitivity to measurement outliers. These findings offer practical guidance for selecting CL algorithms based on application requirements.

</details>


### [208] [NanoBench: A Multi-Task Benchmark Dataset for Nano-Quadrotor System Identification, Control, and State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09908)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NanoBench是首个针对27克纳米四旋翼飞行器（Crazyflie 2.1）的开源多任务基准数据集，提供100Hz的原始传感器数据、控制器内部状态、电机命令和毫米级精度地面真值，用于系统辨识、控制器评估和状态估计研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机器人基准主要针对数百克到几公斤的飞行器，只提供高层状态数据，无法研究纳米级四旋翼特有的低雷诺数空气动力学、无刷直流电机非线性和严重计算约束等问题。

Method: 在Vicon运动捕捉环境中使用Crazyflie 2.1纳米四旋翼（起飞重量27克）收集超过170条飞行轨迹，包括悬停、多频激励、标准跟踪和激进机动。提供100Hz同步数据：Vicon地面真值、原始IMU数据、EKF估计、PID控制器内部状态、电机PWM命令，以及10Hz电池遥测，对齐精度优于0.5毫秒。

Result: 创建了首个公开数据集，联合提供执行器命令、控制器内部状态和估计器输出，具有毫米级精度地面真值。定义了标准化评估协议、训练/测试分割和开源基线，支持三个任务：非线性系统辨识、闭环控制器基准测试和机载状态估计评估。

Conclusion: NanoBench填补了纳米级四旋翼研究的数据空白，为研究低雷诺数空气动力学、电机非线性和计算约束下的控制问题提供了首个全面的基准平台，将促进纳米无人机领域的发展。

Abstract: Existing aerial-robotics benchmarks target vehicles from hundreds of grams to several kilograms and typically expose only high-level state data. They omit the actuator-level signals required to study nano-scale quadrotors, where low-Reynolds number aerodynamics, coreless DC motor nonlinearities, and severe computational constraints invalidate models and controllers developed for larger vehicles. We introduce NanoBench, an open-source multi-task benchmark collected on the commercially available Crazyflie 2.1 nano-quadrotor (takeoff weight 27 g) in a Vicon motion capture arena. The dataset contains over 170 flight trajectories spanning hover, multi-frequency excitation, standard tracking, and aggressive maneuvers across multiple speed regimes. Each trajectory provides synchronized Vicon ground truth, raw IMU data, onboard extended Kalman filter estimates, PID controller internals, and motor PWM commands at 100 Hz, alongside battery telemetry at 10 Hz, aligned with sub-0.5 ms consistency. NanoBench defines standardized evaluation protocols, train/test splits, and open-source baselines for three tasks: nonlinear system identification, closed-loop controller benchmarking, and onboard state estimation assessment. To our knowledge, it is the first public dataset to jointly provide actuator commands, controller internals, and estimator outputs with millimeter-accurate ground truth on a commercially available nano-scale aerial platform.

</details>


### [209] [Kinodynamic Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Locomotion via Multi-Contact Whole-Body Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09956)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Steven Haener,Varun Madabushi,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KDMR是一个新颖的人形机器人运动重定向框架，将重定向建模为多接触全身轨迹优化问题，通过整合动力学约束和地面反作用力测量，生成动态可行的运动轨迹，显著提升下游模仿学习策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动捕捉的纯运动学重定向方法会产生物理不一致的伪影（如脚部滑动和地面穿透），严重影响下游模仿学习策略的性能。需要将动力学约束纳入重定向过程以生成物理可行的运动。

Method: KDMR将重定向建模为多接触全身轨迹优化问题，明确强制执行刚体动力学和接触互补约束。通过整合地面反作用力测量和运动捕捉数据，自动检测脚跟-脚趾接触事件以准确复制复杂的人类接触模式。

Result: KDMR在三个关键维度上显著优于最先进的基线GMR：1）重定向运动的动态可行性和平滑性；2）与原始源数据相比的地面反作用力跟踪准确性；3）通过BeyondMimic框架训练的下游控制策略的训练效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: KDMR框架超越了纯运动学方法，生成动态可行的参考轨迹，加速策略收敛并增强整体运动稳定性。该端到端管道将在发表后开源。

Abstract: We present the KinoDynamic Motion Retargeting (KDMR) framework, a novel approach for humanoid locomotion that models the retargeting process as a multi-contact, whole-body trajectory optimization problem. Conventional kinematics-based retargeting methods rely solely on spatial motion capture (MoCap) data, inevitably introducing physically inconsistent artifacts, such as foot sliding and ground penetration, that severely degrade the performance of downstream imitation learning policies. To bridge this gap, KDMR extends beyond pure kinematics by explicitly enforcing rigid-body dynamics and contact complementarity constraints. Further, by integrating ground reaction force (GRF) measurements alongside MoCap data, our method automatically detects heel-toe contact events to accurately replicate complex human-like contact patterns. We evaluate KDMR against the state-of-the-art baseline, GMR, across three key dimensions: 1) the dynamic feasibility and smoothness of the retargeted motions, 2) the accuracy of GRF tracking compared to raw source data, and 3) the training efficiency and final performance of downstream control policies trained via the BeyondMimic framework. Experimental results demonstrate that KDMR significantly outperforms purely kinematic methods, yielding dynamically viable reference trajectories that accelerate policy convergence and enhance overall locomotion stability. Our end-to-end pipeline will be open-sourced upon publication.

</details>


### [210] [BEACON: Language-Conditioned Navigation Affordance Prediction under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09961)
*Xinyu Gao,Gang Chen,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEACON提出了一种基于鸟瞰图（BEV）的视觉语言空间定位方法，用于解决机器人语言条件本地导航中目标位置被遮挡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的方法通常在图像空间进行推理，产生与可见像素相关的2D预测，难以推断被家具或移动人类遮挡区域的目标位置。

Method: BEACON预测以机器人为中心的局部鸟瞰图（BEV）可通行性热图，包括遮挡区域。通过向VLM注入空间线索，并将VLM输出与深度导出的BEV特征融合，从四个方向的环绕RGB-D观测中预测BEV热图。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器中构建的遮挡感知数据集上进行实验，BEACON在目标位置被遮挡的验证子集上，相对于最先进的图像空间基线，平均地理距离准确率提高了22.74个百分点。

Conclusion: BEV空间表示能有效解决遮挡问题，BEACON方法在语言条件本地导航任务中显著提升了遮挡区域目标定位的准确性。

Abstract: Language-conditioned local navigation requires a robot to infer a nearby traversable target location from its current observation and an open-vocabulary, relational instruction. Existing vision-language spatial grounding methods usually rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to reason in image space, producing 2D predictions tied to visible pixels. As a result, they struggle to infer target locations in occluded regions, typically caused by furniture or moving humans. To address this issue, we propose BEACON, which predicts an ego-centric Bird's-Eye View (BEV) affordance heatmap over a bounded local region including occluded areas. Given an instruction and surround-view RGB-D observations from four directions around the robot, BEACON predicts the BEV heatmap by injecting spatial cues into a VLM and fusing the VLM's output with depth-derived BEV features. Using an occlusion-aware dataset built in the Habitat simulator, we conduct detailed experimental analysis to validate both our BEV space formulation and the design choices of each module. Our method improves the accuracy averaged across geodesic thresholds by 22.74 percentage points over the state-of-the-art image-space baseline on the validation subset with occluded target locations. Our project page is: https://xin-yu-gao.github.io/beacon.

</details>


### [211] [TiPToP: A Modular Open-Vocabulary Planning System for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09971)
*William Shen,Nishanth Kumar,Sahit Chintalapudi,Jie Wang,Christopher Watson,Edward Hu,Jing Cao,Dinesh Jayaraman,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TiPToP是一个结合预训练视觉基础模型与任务运动规划器的模块化系统，无需机器人数据即可通过RGB图像和自然语言指令解决多步骤操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要大量特定机器人的演示数据，而TiPToP旨在构建一个简单易用、无需机器人数据、可快速部署到新机器人平台的模块化操作系统。

Method: 结合预训练视觉基础模型与现有任务运动规划器(TAMP)，采用模块化架构，从RGB图像和自然语言指令直接生成多步骤操作计划。

Result: 在28个桌面操作任务的仿真和真实世界评估中，TiPToP（零机器人数据）匹配或优于经过350小时特定机器人演示微调的π₀.₅-DROID模型。

Conclusion: 模块化架构使系统分析更加透明，为学习与规划的更紧密集成提供了方向，系统已开源以促进模块化操作系统研究。

Abstract: We present TiPToP, an extensible modular system that combines pretrained vision foundation models with an existing Task and Motion Planner (TAMP) to solve multi-step manipulation tasks directly from input RGB images and natural-language instructions. Our system aims to be simple and easy-to-use: it can be installed and run on a standard DROID setup in under one hour and adapted to new embodiments with minimal effort. We evaluate TiPToP -- which requires zero robot data -- over 28 tabletop manipulation tasks in simulation and the real world and find it matches or outperforms $π_{0.5}\text{-DROID}$, a vision-language-action (VLA) model fine-tuned on 350 hours of embodiment-specific demonstrations. TiPToP's modular architecture enables us to analyze the system's failure modes at the component level. We analyze results from an evaluation of 173 trials and identify directions for improvement. We release TiPToP open-source to further research on modular manipulation systems and tighter integration between learning and planning. Project website and code: https://tiptop-robot.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [MASEval: Extending Multi-Agent Evaluation from Models to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08835)
*Cornelius Emde,Alexander Rubinstein,Anmol Goel,Ahmed Heakl,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASEval是一个框架无关的评估库，用于系统级比较LLM智能体系统，发现框架选择与模型选择同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试都是模型中心的，固定智能体设置而不比较其他系统组件。作者认为实现决策（如拓扑结构、编排逻辑、错误处理）会显著影响性能，需要系统级的评估方法。

Method: 开发了MASEval框架无关库，将整个系统作为分析单元。通过系统级比较，涵盖3个基准测试、3个模型和3个框架，全面评估智能体系统性能。

Result: 研究发现框架选择与模型选择同等重要。MASEval允许研究人员探索智能体系统的所有组件，为系统设计提供新途径，帮助从业者为特定用例选择最佳实现。

Conclusion: MASEval填补了智能体系统评估的空白，提供了框架无关的系统级分析方法，对研究和实践都有重要意义。该工具在MIT许可下开源。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of LLM-based agentic systems has produced a rich ecosystem of frameworks (smolagents, LangGraph, AutoGen, CAMEL, LlamaIndex, i.a.). Yet existing benchmarks are model-centric: they fix the agentic setup and do not compare other system components. We argue that implementation decisions substantially impact performance, including choices such as topology, orchestration logic, and error handling. MASEval addresses this evaluation gap with a framework-agnostic library that treats the entire system as the unit of analysis. Through a systematic system-level comparison across 3 benchmarks, 3 models, and 3 frameworks, we find that framework choice matters as much as model choice. MASEval allows researchers to explore all components of agentic systems, opening new avenues for principled system design, and practitioners to identify the best implementation for their use case.
  MASEval is available under the MIT licence https://github.com/parameterlab/MASEval.

</details>


### [213] [LDP: An Identity-Aware Protocol for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08852)
*Sunil Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM Delegate Protocol (LDP) 是一个AI原生通信协议，通过引入身份卡、渐进载荷、治理会话、结构化溯源和信任域等机制，解决了现有协议无法暴露模型级属性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多智能体AI系统协议（如A2A和MCP）无法将模型级属性作为一等公民暴露，忽略了有效委托所需的基本属性：模型身份、推理配置、质量校准和成本特征。

Method: 提出LLM Delegate Protocol (LDP)，包含五个机制：1) 丰富的委托身份卡；2) 渐进载荷模式；3) 治理会话；4) 结构化溯源跟踪；5) 信任域。在JamJet代理运行时中实现为插件，使用本地Ollama模型和LLM-as-judge评估。

Result: 身份感知路由在简单任务上实现约12倍延迟降低；语义帧载荷减少37%令牌数；治理会话在10轮对话中消除39%令牌开销；噪声溯源会降低合成质量。模拟分析显示攻击检测（96% vs 6%）和故障恢复（100% vs 35%）方面的架构优势。

Conclusion: LDP协议设计、参考实现和初步证据表明，AI原生协议原语能够实现更高效和可治理的委托。

Abstract: As multi-agent AI systems grow in complexity, the protocols connecting them constrain their capabilities. Current protocols such as A2A and MCP do not expose model-level properties as first-class primitives, ignoring properties fundamental to effective delegation: model identity, reasoning profile, quality calibration, and cost characteristics. We present the LLM Delegate Protocol (LDP), an AI-native communication protocol introducing five mechanisms: (1) rich delegate identity cards with quality hints and reasoning profiles; (2) progressive payload modes with negotiation and fallback; (3) governed sessions with persistent context; (4) structured provenance tracking confidence and verification status; (5) trust domains enforcing security boundaries at the protocol level. We implement LDP as a plugin for the JamJet agent runtime and evaluate against A2A and random baselines using local Ollama models and LLM-as-judge evaluation. Identity-aware routing achieves ~12x lower latency on easy tasks through delegate specialization, though it does not improve aggregate quality in our small delegate pool; semantic frame payloads reduce token count by 37% (p=0.031) with no observed quality loss; governed sessions eliminate 39% token overhead at 10 rounds; and noisy provenance degrades synthesis quality below the no-provenance baseline, arguing that confidence metadata is harmful without verification. Simulated analyses show architectural advantages in attack detection (96% vs. 6%) and failure recovery (100% vs. 35% completion). This paper contributes a protocol design, reference implementation, and initial evidence that AI-native protocol primitives enable more efficient and governable delegation.

</details>


### [214] [Quantifying the Accuracy and Cost Impact of Design Decisions in Budget-Constrained Agentic LLM Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08877)
*Kyle McCleary,James Ghawaly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Budget-Constrained Agentic Search (BCAS)框架，研究在固定预算约束下搜索深度、检索策略和完成预算对智能体RAG系统准确性和成本的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体检索增强生成(RAG)系统结合了迭代搜索、规划提示和检索后端，但在实际部署中需要对工具调用和完成令牌设置明确的预算限制。目前缺乏在固定约束下系统研究这些因素如何影响准确性和成本的研究。

Method: 提出BCAS评估框架，这是一个模型无关的评估工具，能够监控剩余预算并控制工具使用。在6个LLM和3个问答基准上进行比较实验，系统研究搜索深度、检索策略（混合词法和密集检索+轻量级重排序）和完成预算的影响。

Result: 1) 准确性随搜索次数增加而提高，但存在小的上限；2) 混合词法和密集检索配合轻量级重排序在消融实验中带来最大平均增益；3) 更大的完成预算在HotpotQA风格的合成任务中最有帮助。

Conclusion: 研究结果为配置预算受限的智能体检索管道提供了实用指导，并提供了可复现的提示和评估设置。结果表明在预算约束下，适度的搜索深度、混合检索策略和针对任务类型的预算分配是最优配置。

Abstract: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems combine iterative search, planning prompts, and retrieval backends, but deployed settings impose explicit budgets on tool calls and completion tokens. We present a controlled measurement study of how search depth, retrieval strategy, and completion budget affect accuracy and cost under fixed constraints. Using Budget-Constrained Agentic Search (BCAS), a model-agnostic evaluation harness that surfaces remaining budget and gates tool use, we run comparisons across six LLMs and three question-answering benchmarks. Across models and datasets, accuracy improves with additional searches up to a small cap, hybrid lexical and dense retrieval with lightweight re-ranking produces the largest average gains in our ablation grid, and larger completion budgets are most helpful on HotpotQA-style synthesis. These results provide practical guidance for configuring budgeted agentic retrieval pipelines and are accompanied by reproducible prompts and evaluation settings.

</details>


### [215] [Interpretable Markov-Based Spatiotemporal Risk Surfaces for Missing-Child Search Planning with Reinforcement Learning and LLM-Based Quality Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08933)
*Joshua Castillo,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guardian系统为失踪儿童案件提供端到端决策支持，通过三层预测架构（马尔可夫链、强化学习、LLM验证）生成0-72小时概率搜索计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失踪儿童案件的前72小时对成功找回至关重要，但执法机构面临数据碎片化、非结构化以及缺乏动态地理空间预测工具的问题。

Method: 1. 将异构非结构化案件文档转换为时空表示；2. 三层预测架构：第一层马尔可夫链（考虑道路可达性、隐蔽偏好、走廊偏差等），第二层强化学习优化搜索计划，第三层LLM进行事后验证。

Result: 系统在24/48/72小时时间范围内生成定量输出，分析敏感性、失败模式和权衡，为区域优化和人工审查提供可解释的先验概率。

Conclusion: Guardian系统通过三层预测架构为失踪儿童调查提供可解释、可操作的搜索计划，支持执法机构在关键72小时内做出更有效的决策。

Abstract: The first 72 hours of a missing-child investigation are critical for successful recovery. However, law enforcement agencies often face fragmented, unstructured data and a lack of dynamic, geospatial predictive tools. Our system, Guardian, provides an end-to-end decision-support system for missing-child investigation and early search planning. It converts heterogeneous, unstructured case documents into a schema-aligned spatiotemporal representation, enriches cases with geocoding and transportation context, and provides probabilistic search products spanning 0-72 hours. In this paper, we present an overview of Guardian as well as a detailed description of a three-layer predictive component of the system. The first layer is a Markov chain, a sparse, interpretable model with transitions incorporating road accessibility costs, seclusion preferences, and corridor bias with separate day/night parameterizations. The Markov chain's output prediction distributions are then transformed into operationally useful search plans by the second layer's reinforcement learning. Finally, the third layer's LLM performs post hoc validation of layer 2 search plans prior to their release. Using a synthetic but realistic case study, we report quantitative outputs across 24/48/72-hour horizons and analyze sensitivity, failure modes, and tradeoffs. Results show that the proposed predictive system with the three-layer architecture produces interpretable priors for zone optimization and human review.

</details>


### [216] [AgentOS: From Application Silos to a Natural Language-Driven Data Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08938)
*Rui Liu,Tao Zhe,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Kunpeng Liu,Yanjie Fu,Huan Liu,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentOS——一种以自然语言为中心的个人智能体操作系统，旨在解决当前LLM智能体在传统操作系统架构下的交互碎片化、权限管理和上下文割裂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源本地智能体（如OpenClaw）虽然能自主操作本地环境，但仍运行在传统GUI/CLI操作系统上，导致：1）交互模型碎片化；2）权限管理混乱（"影子AI"问题）；3）严重上下文割裂。需要新的操作系统范式来支持智能体原生交互。

Method: 提出AgentOS架构：1）用自然用户界面（NUI）替代传统GUI桌面；2）核心是Agent Kernel，负责解析用户意图、分解任务、协调多个智能体；3）传统应用演变为模块化"技能即模块"，用户可通过自然语言规则组合软件。

Result: 将AgentOS的实现重新定义为知识发现与数据挖掘（KDD）问题：Agent Kernel作为实时意图挖掘和知识发现引擎，操作系统成为连续数据挖掘管道，涉及工作流自动化的序列模式挖掘、技能检索的推荐系统、动态演化的个人知识图谱。

Conclusion: 实现AgentOS需要解决KDD领域的核心挑战，这为KDD社区定义了新的研究议程，以构建下一代智能计算系统。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of open-source, locally hosted intelligent agents marks a critical inflection point in human-computer interaction. Systems such as OpenClaw demonstrate that Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents can autonomously operate local computing environments, orchestrate workflows, and integrate external tools. However, within the current paradigm, these agents remain conventional applications running on legacy operating systems originally designed for Graphical User Interfaces (GUIs) or Command Line Interfaces (CLIs). This architectural mismatch leads to fragmented interaction models, poorly structured permission management (often described as "Shadow AI"), and severe context fragmentation. This paper proposes a new paradigm: a Personal Agent Operating System (AgentOS). In AgentOS, traditional GUI desktops are replaced by a Natural User Interface (NUI) centered on a unified natural language or voice portal. The system core becomes an Agent Kernel that interprets user intent, decomposes tasks, and coordinates multiple agents, while traditional applications evolve into modular Skills-as-Modules enabling users to compose software through natural language rules. We argue that realizing AgentOS fundamentally becomes a Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining (KDD) problem. The Agent Kernel must operate as a real-time engine for intent mining and knowledge discovery. Viewed through this lens, the operating system becomes a continuous data mining pipeline involving sequential pattern mining for workflow automation, recommender systems for skill retrieval, and dynamically evolving personal knowledge graphs. These challenges define a new research agenda for the KDD community in building the next generation of intelligent computing systems.

</details>


### [217] [A Consensus-Driven Multi-LLM Pipeline for Missing-Person Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08954)
*Joshua Castillo,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guardian LLM Pipeline：用于失踪儿童调查的多模型系统，通过LLM进行智能信息提取和处理，采用共识机制解决模型分歧，并通过QLoRA微调增强性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失踪人员调查的前72小时对成功找回至关重要，需要高效的信息处理和搜索规划支持系统

Method: 构建多模型LLM管道，协调任务专用LLM模型，引入共识LLM引擎比较多个模型输出并解决分歧，使用QLoRA微调技术基于精选数据集优化性能

Result: 开发了Guardian端到端系统，支持失踪儿童调查和早期搜索规划，将LLM作为结构化提取器和标注器使用，而非无约束的端到端决策者

Conclusion: Guardian LLM Pipeline通过保守、可审计的方式使用LLM，强调弱监督和LLM辅助标注，为关键失踪人员调查提供可靠的信息处理支持

Abstract: The first 72 hours of a missing-person investigation are critical for successful recovery. Guardian is an end-to-end system designed to support missing-child investigation and early search planning. This paper presents the Guardian LLM Pipeline, a multi-model system in which LLMs are used for intelligent information extraction and processing related to missing-person search operations. The pipeline coordinates end-to-end execution across task-specialized LLM models and invokes a consensus LLM engine that compares multiple model outputs and resolves disagreements. The pipeline is further strengthened by QLoRA-based fine-tuning, using curated datasets. The presented design aligns with prior work on weak supervision and LLM-assisted annotation, emphasizing conservative, auditable use of LLMs as structured extractors and labelers rather than unconstrained end-to-end decision makers.

</details>


### [218] [The FABRIC Strategy for Verifying Neural Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08964)
*I. Samuel Akinwande,Sydney M. Katz,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FaBRIC算法，结合前向和后向可达性分析来验证神经反馈系统的可达-规避规范，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经反馈系统的后向可达性分析研究较少，现有技术可扩展性有限，需要开发更有效的后向可达性分析方法。

Method: 提出新算法计算非线性神经反馈系统后向可达集的过近似和欠近似，并将后向可达性技术与现有前向分析技术集成，形成FaBRIC算法。

Result: 在代表性基准测试中，新算法显著优于现有技术水平，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FaBRIC算法成功整合前向和后向可达性分析，为神经反馈系统的验证提供了更强大的工具，填补了后向可达性分析的研究空白。

Abstract: Forward reachability analysis is a dominant approach for verifying reach-avoid specifications in neural feedback systems, i.e., dynamical systems controlled by neural networks, and a number of directions have been proposed and studied. In contrast, far less attention has been given to backward reachability analysis for these systems, in part because of the limited scalability of known techniques. In this work, we begin to address this gap by introducing new algorithms for computing both over- and underapproximations of backward reachable sets for nonlinear neural feedback systems. We also describe and implement an integration of these backward reachability techniques with existing ones for forward analysis. We call the resulting algorithm Forward and Backward Reachability Integration for Certification (FaBRIC). We evaluate our algorithms on a representative set of benchmarks and show that they significantly outperform the prior state of the art.

</details>


### [219] [Meissa: Multi-modal Medical Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09018)
*Yixiong Chen,Xinyi Bai,Yue Pan,Zongwei Zhou,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meissa是一个4B参数的轻量级医疗多模态大语言模型，通过蒸馏前沿模型的结构化轨迹实现离线智能体能力，在医疗图像理解和临床推理任务中性能媲美前沿模型，同时大幅降低成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗智能体系统过度依赖GPT等前沿模型，存在API部署成本高、延迟大、隐私风险等问题，无法满足临床现场部署需求。需要开发轻量级、可离线运行的医疗智能体模型。

Method: 1. 统一轨迹建模：将推理和行动轨迹表示为单一的状态-行动-观察形式，使模型能泛化到异构医疗环境；2. 三层分层监督：根据模型自身错误触发从直接推理到工具增强和多智能体交互的渐进升级；3. 前瞻-回顾监督：将探索性前向轨迹与事后合理化执行轨迹配对，稳定学习交互策略。

Result: 在40K精选轨迹上训练后，Meissa在13个医疗基准测试的16个评估设置中，有10个匹配或超过专有前沿智能体。相比Gemini-3等前沿模型，参数减少25倍以上，端到端延迟降低22倍，完全离线运行。

Conclusion: Meissa证明了通过结构化轨迹蒸馏前沿模型知识，可以构建轻量级、高性能的离线医疗智能体，解决了现有系统在成本、延迟和隐私方面的限制，为临床现场部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MM-LLMs) have shown strong performance in medical image understanding and clinical reasoning. Recent medical agent systems extend them with tool use and multi-agent collaboration, enabling complex decision-making. However, these systems rely almost entirely on frontier models (e.g., GPT), whose API-based deployment incurs high cost, high latency, and privacy risks that conflict with on-premise clinical requirements. We present Meissa, a lightweight 4B-parameter medical MM-LLM that brings agentic capability offline. Instead of imitating static answers, Meissa learns both when to engage external interaction (strategy selection) and how to execute multi-step interaction (strategy execution) by distilling structured trajectories from frontier models. Specifically, we propose: (1) Unified trajectory modeling: trajectories (reasoning and action traces) are represented within a single state-action-observation formalism, allowing one model to generalize across heterogeneous medical environments. (2) Three-tier stratified supervision: the model's own errors trigger progressive escalation from direct reasoning to tool-augmented and multi-agent interaction, explicitly learning difficulty-aware strategy selection. (3) Prospective-retrospective supervision: pairing exploratory forward traces with hindsight-rationalized execution traces enables stable learning of effective interaction policies. Trained on 40K curated trajectories, Meissa matches or exceeds proprietary frontier agents in 10 of 16 evaluation settings across 13 medical benchmarks spanning radiology, pathology, and clinical reasoning. Using over 25x fewer parameters than typical frontier models like Gemini-3, Meissa operates fully offline with 22x lower end-to-end latency compared to API-based deployment. Data, models, and environments are released at https://github.com/Schuture/Meissa.

</details>


### [220] [MEMO: Memory-Augmented Model Context Optimization for Robust Multi-Turn Multi-Agent LLM Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09022)
*Yunfei Xie,Kevin Wang,Bobby Cheng,Jianzhu Yao,Zhizhou Sha,Alexander Duffy,Yihan Xi,Hongyuan Mei,Cheston Tan,Chen Wei,Pramod Viswanath,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEMO框架通过记忆增强和探索优化多智能体LLM游戏评估，显著提升胜率并降低方差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮多智能体LLM游戏评估存在显著的运行间方差问题，早期微小偏差会通过多智能体耦合在长期交互中被放大，导致胜率估计偏差和排名不可靠。提示选择进一步加剧了这一问题，因为不同提示会产生不同的有效策略。

Method: MEMO（记忆增强模型上下文优化）是一个自博弈框架，通过耦合保留和探索来优化推理时上下文。保留机制维护一个持久记忆库，存储自博弈轨迹的结构化洞察，并在后续游戏中作为先验注入。探索机制运行锦标赛式提示进化，通过TrueSkill进行不确定性感知选择，并使用优先回放重新访问罕见和决定性状态。

Result: 在五个文本游戏中，MEMO将GPT-4o-mini的平均胜率从25.1%提升到49.5%，将Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct的平均胜率从20.9%提升到44.3%（每个任务使用2000个自博弈游戏）。运行间方差也显著降低，使得不同提示变体下的排名更加稳定。在谈判和不完全信息游戏中获得最大提升，而在完全信息设置中强化学习仍然更有效。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM游戏性能和鲁棒性通过上下文优化还有很大的提升空间。MEMO框架通过记忆增强和探索优化，显著提高了胜率并降低了评估方差，为多智能体LLM游戏评估提供了更稳定可靠的方法。

Abstract: Multi-turn, multi-agent LLM game evaluations often exhibit substantial run-to-run variance. In long-horizon interactions, small early deviations compound across turns and are amplified by multi-agent coupling. This biases win rate estimates and makes rankings unreliable across repeated tournaments. Prompt choice worsens this further by producing different effective policies. We address both instability and underperformance with MEMO (Memory-augmented MOdel context optimization), a self-play framework that optimizes inference-time context by coupling retention and exploration. Retention maintains a persistent memory bank that stores structured insights from self-play trajectories and injects them as priors during later play. Exploration runs tournament-style prompt evolution with uncertainty-aware selection via TrueSkill, and uses prioritized replay to revisit rare and decisive states. Across five text-based games, MEMO raises mean win rate from 25.1% to 49.5% for GPT-4o-mini and from 20.9% to 44.3% for Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, using $2,000$ self-play games per task. Run-to-run variance also drops, giving more stable rankings across prompt variations. These results suggest that multi-agent LLM game performance and robustness have substantial room for improvement through context optimization. MEMO achieves the largest gains in negotiation and imperfect-information games, while RL remains more effective in perfect-information settings.

</details>


### [221] [Time, Identity and Consciousness in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09043)
*Elija Perrier,Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Stack Theory的机器意识评估框架，通过分离语言行为与决策约束，量化身份稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型代理的意识评估主要依赖行为观察（语言和工具使用），但代理可能在决策时缺乏使身份陈述有意义的约束条件，导致"说对的话"与"真实身份组织"之间的脱节

Method: 应用Stack Theory的时间间隙概念来构建轨迹脚手架，分离评估窗口内的成分出现与单一目标步骤中的共同实例化；实例化Stack Theory的Arpeggio和Chord假设于基础身份陈述，从仪器化脚手架轨迹计算两个持久性分数

Result: 开发出两个可计算的持久性分数，连接到五个操作性身份指标，将常见脚手架映射到身份形态空间，揭示可预测的权衡关系

Conclusion: 创建了一个保守的身份评估工具包，能够区分"像稳定自我一样说话"与"像稳定自我一样组织"，为机器意识评估提供更严谨的方法

Abstract: Machine consciousness evaluations mostly see behavior. For language model agents that behavior is language and tool use. That lets an agent say the right things about itself even when the constraints that should make those statements matter are not jointly present at decision time. We apply Stack Theory's temporal gap to scaffold trajectories. This separates ingredient-wise occurrence within an evaluation window from co-instantiation at a single objective step. We then instantiate Stack Theory's Arpeggio and Chord postulates on grounded identity statements. This yields two persistence scores that can be computed from instrumented scaffold traces. We connect these scores to five operational identity metrics and map common scaffolds into an identity morphospace that exposes predictable tradeoffs. The result is a conservative toolkit for identity evaluation. It separates talking like a stable self from being organized like one.

</details>


### [222] [EPOCH: An Agentic Protocol for Multi-Round System Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09049)
*Zhanlin Liu,Yitao Li,Munirathnam Srikanth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPOCH是一个用于多轮系统优化的工程协议，通过标准化角色、阶段和接口来协调提示、代码和模型配置的自主改进，确保稳定性、可重现性和可追踪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主代理方法通常是针对特定任务的优化循环，缺乏统一协议来建立基准和管理多轮自我改进。需要一种能协调异构环境中多组件优化的标准化方法。

Method: EPOCH协议将优化分为两个阶段：基准构建和迭代自我改进。每轮通过角色约束的阶段（规划、实现、评估）进行结构化，使用规范命令接口和轮级跟踪来标准化执行。

Result: 在各种任务中的实证研究表明，EPOCH适用于面向生产的自主改进工作流程，能够协调提示、模型配置、代码和基于规则组件的优化，同时保持评估的稳定性、可重现性、可追踪性和完整性。

Conclusion: EPOCH提供了一个统一的工程协议，用于管理多轮系统优化，解决了现有方法缺乏标准化和协调性的问题，为生产环境中的自主改进提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly used to improve prompts, code, and machine learning systems through iterative execution and feedback. Yet existing approaches are usually designed as task-specific optimization loops rather than as a unified protocol for establishing baselines and managing tracked multi-round self-improvement. We introduce EPOCH, an engineering protocol for multi-round system optimization in heterogeneous environments. EPOCH organizes optimization into two phases: baseline construction and iterative self-improvement. It further structures each round through role-constrained stages that separate planning, implementation, and evaluation, and standardizes execution through canonical command interfaces and round-level tracking. This design enables coordinated optimization across prompts, model configurations, code, and rule-based components while preserving stability, reproducibility, traceability, and integrity of evaluation. Empirical studies in various tasks illustrate the practicality of EPOCH for production-oriented autonomous improvement workflows.

</details>


### [223] [From Days to Minutes: An Autonomous AI Agent Achieves Reliable Clinical Triage in Remote Patient Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09052)
*Seunghwan Kim,Tiffany H. Kung,Heena Verma,Dilan Edirisinghe,Kaveh Sedehi,Johanna Alvarez,Diane Shilling,Audra Lisa Doyle,Ajit Chary,William Borden,Ming Jack Po*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sentinel AI agent使用MCP协议和21个临床工具实现RPM生命体征自主分诊，在紧急情况敏感性(95.8%)和可行动警报敏感性(88.5%)上超越个体临床医生，每次分诊成本仅0.34美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远程患者监测(RPM)产生海量数据，但临床试验(如Tele-HF、BEAT-HF)因数据量过大压垮临床人员而失败。虽然TIM-HF2显示24/7医生主导监测能降低30%死亡率，但该模式成本过高且难以扩展。

Method: 开发Sentinel自主AI代理，使用模型上下文协议(MCP)通过21个临床工具和多步推理进行RPM生命体征的上下文分诊。评估包括：(1)自我一致性测试；(2)与基于规则的阈值比较；(3)通过连接矩阵设计与6名临床医生(3名医生、3名执业护士)验证，并进行留一分析。

Result: 与人类多数投票标准(N=467)相比，代理达到95.8%紧急情况敏感性和88.5%可行动警报敏感性。在留一分析中，代理在紧急敏感性(97.5% vs 60.0%)和可行动敏感性(90.9% vs 69.5%)上超越所有临床医生。自我一致性接近完美(kappa=0.850)，每次分诊中位成本0.34美元。

Conclusion: Sentinel以超越个体临床医生的敏感性分诊RPM生命体征。通过自动化系统上下文合成，Sentinel解决了先前RPM试验的核心限制，为降低死亡率的强化监测提供了可扩展路径，同时保持临床可辩护的过度分诊特征。

Abstract: Background: Remote patient monitoring (RPM) generates vast data, yet landmark trials (Tele-HF, BEAT-HF) failed because data volume overwhelmed clinical staff. While TIM-HF2 showed 24/7 physician-led monitoring reduces mortality by 30%, this model remains prohibitively expensive and unscalable.
  Methods: We developed Sentinel, an autonomous AI agent using Model Context Protocol (MCP) for contextual triage of RPM vitals via 21 clinical tools and multi-step reasoning. Evaluation included: (1) self-consistency (100 readings x 5 runs); (2) comparison against rule-based thresholds; and (3) validation against 6 clinicians (3 physicians, 3 NPs) using a connected matrix design. A leave-one-out (LOO) analysis compared the agent against individual clinicians; severe overtriage cases underwent independent physician adjudication.
  Results: Against a human majority-vote standard (N=467), the agent achieved 95.8% emergency sensitivity and 88.5% sensitivity for all actionable alerts (85.7% specificity). Four-level exact accuracy was 69.4% (quadratic-weighted kappa=0.778); 95.9% of classifications were within one severity level. In LOO analysis, the agent outperformed every clinician in emergency sensitivity (97.5% vs. 60.0% aggregate) and actionable sensitivity (90.9% vs. 69.5%). While disagreements skewed toward overtriage (22.5%), independent adjudication of severe gaps (>=2 levels) validated agent escalation in 88-94% of cases; consensus resolution validated 100%. The agent showed near-perfect self-consistency (kappa=0.850). Median cost was $0.34/triage.
  Conclusions: Sentinel triages RPM vitals with sensitivity exceeding individual clinicians. By automating systematic context synthesis, Sentinel addresses the core limitation of prior RPM trials, offering a scalable path toward the intensive monitoring shown to reduce mortality while maintaining a clinically defensible overtriage profile.

</details>


### [224] [Chaotic Dynamics in Multi-LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09127)
*Hajime Shimao,Warut Khern-am-nuai,Sung Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多LLM委员会作为随机动力系统的稳定性，发现即使在T=0条件下，角色分化和模型异质性都会导致系统不稳定，需要将稳定性审计作为多LLM治理系统的核心设计需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体AI系统越来越依赖多LLM审议，但其在重复执行下的稳定性特征仍不清楚。需要量化多LLM委员会作为随机动力系统的稳定性，特别是在实践中常被期望具有确定性行为的T=0条件下。

Method: 将五智能体LLM委员会建模为随机动力系统，使用经验Lyapunov指数（$\hatλ$）量化轨迹发散。在12个政策场景中进行因子设计，分析角色分化和模型异质性对稳定性的影响。通过主席角色消融和缩短记忆窗口等协议变体进行机制分析。

Result: 发现两个独立的失稳路径：同质委员会中的角色分化和无角色委员会中的模型异质性。在HL-01基准测试中，两种路径都产生较高的发散度（$\hatλ=0.0541$和$0.0947$）。混合+角色条件的失稳程度低于混合+无角色条件（$\hatλ=0.0519$ vs $0.0947$），显示非加性交互作用。主席角色消融最能降低$\hatλ$，缩短记忆窗口进一步减弱发散。

Conclusion: 即使在T=0条件下，多LLM委员会也会表现出显著的随机性，稳定性审计应成为多LLM治理系统的核心设计需求。角色分配和模型选择对系统稳定性有重要影响，需要针对性设计协议来增强稳定性。

Abstract: Collective AI systems increasingly rely on multi-LLM deliberation, but their stability under repeated execution remains poorly characterized. We model five-agent LLM committees as random dynamical systems and quantify inter-run sensitivity using an empirical Lyapunov exponent ($\hatλ$) derived from trajectory divergence in committee mean preferences. Across 12 policy scenarios, a factorial design at $T=0$ identifies two independent routes to instability: role differentiation in homogeneous committees and model heterogeneity in no-role committees. Critically, these effects appear even in the $T=0$ regime where practitioners often expect deterministic behavior. In the HL-01 benchmark, both routes produce elevated divergence ($\hatλ=0.0541$ and $0.0947$, respectively), while homogeneous no-role committees also remain in a positive-divergence regime ($\hatλ=0.0221$). The combined mixed+roles condition is less unstable than mixed+no-role ($\hatλ=0.0519$ vs $0.0947$), showing non-additive interaction. Mechanistically, Chair-role ablation reduces $\hatλ$ most strongly, and targeted protocol variants that shorten memory windows further attenuate divergence. These results support stability auditing as a core design requirement for multi-LLM governance systems.

</details>


### [225] [Deep Tabular Research via Continual Experience-Driven Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09151)
*Junnan Dong,Chuang Zhou,Zheng Yuan,Yifei Yu,Siyu An,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Feiyue Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DTR框架，通过分层元图、期望感知选择策略和孪生结构化记忆，解决大语言模型在复杂非结构化表格上的长时程推理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理具有分层双向表头和非规范布局的复杂非结构化表格时，难以完成长时程分析任务，需要多步推理和表格区域间的依赖关系处理。

Method: 1) 构建分层元图捕获双向语义，将自然语言查询映射到操作级搜索空间；2) 引入期望感知选择策略优先选择高效用执行路径；3) 使用孪生结构化记忆（参数化更新和抽象文本）合成历史执行结果，实现持续优化。

Result: 在具有挑战性的非结构化表格基准测试中验证了有效性，证明了将战略规划与低级执行分离对于长时程表格推理的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出的代理框架将表格推理视为闭环决策过程，通过耦合查询和表格理解进行路径决策和操作执行，显著提升了复杂表格分析能力。

Abstract: Large language models often struggle with complex long-horizon analytical tasks over unstructured tables, which typically feature hierarchical and bidirectional headers and non-canonical layouts. We formalize this challenge as Deep Tabular Research (DTR), requiring multi-step reasoning over interdependent table regions. To address DTR, we propose a novel agentic framework that treats tabular reasoning as a closed-loop decision-making process. We carefully design a coupled query and table comprehension for path decision making and operational execution. Specifically, (i) DTR first constructs a hierarchical meta graph to capture bidirectional semantics, mapping natural language queries into an operation-level search space; (ii) To navigate this space, we introduce an expectation-aware selection policy that prioritizes high-utility execution paths; (iii) Crucially, historical execution outcomes are synthesized into a siamese structured memory, i.e., parameterized updates and abstracted texts, enabling continual refinement. Extensive experiments on challenging unstructured tabular benchmarks verify the effectiveness and highlight the necessity of separating strategic planning from low-level execution for long-horizon tabular reasoning.

</details>


### [226] [DataFactory: Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Advanced Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09152)
*Tong Wang,Chi Jin,Yongkang Chen,Huan Deng,Xiaohui Kuang,Gang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataFactory是一个多智能体框架，通过专门的团队协调和自动知识转换来解决表格问答中的上下文限制、幻觉问题和复杂推理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在表格问答中存在三个关键限制：上下文长度约束限制数据处理能力、幻觉问题影响答案可靠性、单智能体架构难以处理涉及语义关系和多重逻辑的复杂推理场景。

Method: 提出DataFactory多智能体框架，包含采用ReAct范式进行推理编排的数据领导者，以及专门的数据库和知识图谱团队。通过映射函数T:D x S x R -> G实现自动数据到知识图谱的转换，支持自然语言咨询和灵活的多智能体协商，并应用上下文工程策略整合历史模式和领域知识。

Result: 在TabFact、WikiTableQuestions和FeTaQA数据集上，使用5个提供商的8个LLM，结果显示：TabFact准确率提升20.2%，WikiTQ提升23.9%（Cohen's d > 1）。团队协调优于单团队变体（TabFact +5.5%，WikiTQ +14.4%，FeTaQA ROUGE-2 +17.1%）。

Conclusion: DataFactory框架为多智能体协作提供了设计指南，并通过集成的结构化查询和图知识表示为企业数据分析提供了实用平台，有效解决了表格问答中的关键限制。

Abstract: Table Question Answering (TableQA) enables natural language interaction with structured tabular data. However, existing large language model (LLM) approaches face critical limitations: context length constraints that restrict data handling capabilities, hallucination issues that compromise answer reliability, and single-agent architectures that struggle with complex reasoning scenarios involving semantic relationships and multi-hop logic. This paper introduces DataFactory, a multi-agent framework that addresses these limitations through specialized team coordination and automated knowledge transformation. The framework comprises a Data Leader employing the ReAct paradigm for reasoning orchestration, together with dedicated Database and Knowledge Graph teams, enabling the systematic decomposition of complex queries into structured and relational reasoning tasks. We formalize automated data-to-knowledge graph transformation via the mapping function T:D x S x R -> G, and implement natural language-based consultation that - unlike fixed workflow multi-agent systems - enables flexible inter-agent deliberation and adaptive planning to improve coordination robustness. We also apply context engineering strategies that integrate historical patterns and domain knowledge to reduce hallucinations and improve query accuracy. Across TabFact, WikiTableQuestions, and FeTaQA, using eight LLMs from five providers, results show consistent gains. Our approach improves accuracy by 20.2% (TabFact) and 23.9% (WikiTQ) over baselines, with significant effects (Cohen's d > 1).
